- **agent-handoff**: Moves a live coding-agent session, with its history, from this laptop onto a Leverage cloud machine where it keeps going or a teammate picks it up. Use when the user says "continue this Claude Code session elsewhere", "move this session off my laptop", "let my teammate pick this up", or "hand off this session". Not for writing a handoff summary document. ([leverage-computer/skills](https://github.com/leverage-computer/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Automatically preserve substantive coding work in a local Git repository with the handoff CLI. Use for implementation, debugging, testing, or review work that may outlast one agent; do not use for brief answers, translations, or read-only questions with no repository work. ([lup49488/agent-handoff](https://github.com/lup49488/agent-handoff/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Chiusura sessione. Aggiorna skill se serve, crea report di sessione, archivia plan. Obbligatoria a fine sessione. ([Matteo-Exp-Transformer/Trade-analyst-agent](https://github.com/Matteo-Exp-Transformer/Trade-analyst-agent/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/agent-handoff) · 2 copies)
- **agent-handoff**: Compact the current conversation into a handoff document for another agent to pick up. ([mithril-studio/agent-skills](https://github.com/mithril-studio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/productivity-skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Use at the start and end of every delegated task to report status, modified files, decisions, test results, known issues and next action, and to update the task board with evidence-based states. ([momchilantonov/ai_assisted_development_exam_expense_tracker](https://github.com/momchilantonov/ai_assisted_development_exam_expense_tracker/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Create a concise continuation brief so another agent or future session can pick up the work with context, constraints, next steps, and relevant files. Use when the user asks for a handoff, agent handoff, session summary, continuation brief, next-agent brief, or wants to resume work later. ([MondoBoricua/agent-forge-skills](https://github.com/MondoBoricua/agent-forge-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/productivity/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Write or resume a structured handoff note when work moves between OMP conversations or Herdr panes — "hand off this session", "write a handoff note", "resume from handoff", or ending a session whose remaining work another conversation will pick up. Not for durable project facts (use the owning repository's docs). ([monoclonalAb/dotfiles](https://github.com/monoclonalAb/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/pi/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Produce a compact, copy-paste handoff for a fresh agent that captures the current task, goals, decisions, files, commands, edits, blockers, and next steps without forcing the next agent to rediscover context. Use when the user asks for a summary, handoff, transfer note, next-agent context, session recap for continuation, or a low-token context package for a new chat or agent. ([nolinnguyen/skills](https://github.com/nolinnguyen/skills/tree/HEAD/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Hand the current conversation off to a fresh background agent that picks up the work immediately. ([Non-invasive-Neuromodulation-Lab/skills](https://github.com/Non-invasive-Neuromodulation-Lab/skills/tree/HEAD/agent-handoff) · 2 copies)
- **agent-handoff**: Writes actionable handoffs with a state-checking preflight, essential context, and dependency-ordered actions with completion criteria. Use only when explicitly asked to write a handoff, brief the next session, or package work for a subagent or person. Not for conversation summaries, status or standup updates, plans for unstarted work, commit messages, or PR descriptions. ([nthplusio/skills](https://github.com/nthplusio/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/scoussens-skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Let a site's on-page agent hand a conversation to another agent (A2A 1.0) or to a person on the team, through a relay on the site's own origin that holds the credentials, the target allowlist and the record of what was shared. Use when a site running @nxisai/agent-harness should pass visitors to a booking agent, a partner's agent, a help desk or a Discord channel. ([nxisai/skills](https://github.com/nxisai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Write, pick up, and clear a single shared handoff file between agent sessions. Use only when the user explicitly invokes this skill by name (for example, "$agent-handoff") or explicitly asks to write/read/clear the shared handoff file. Do not use for normal tasks or implicit context transfer. ([Prowlin/profile-configs](https://github.com/Prowlin/profile-configs/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: How the main session briefs the plugin's agents and checks their reports. Use when dispatching any of the plugin's agents (researcher, architect, planner, builder, reviewers, debugger and so on) or reading an agent's report. ([rajubhimani/developer-plugin](https://github.com/rajubhimani/developer-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Use when starting OR ending any work session in this repo as an AI agent (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Cursor, Gemini/Antigravity, human) — establishes the multi-agent handoff protocol check docs/handoffs/ for open work, claim milestone ownership, verify incoming claims before trusting them, and leave an append-only handoff record when stopping. Triggers on "handoff", "take over", "continue from", "codex worked here", "bàn giao", "tiếp tục công việc của agent khác". ([RealDangTan/Happynest](https://github.com/RealDangTan/Happynest/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Use when unfinished authorized work of any kind (including code, review, UAT, or decisions) must continue in another session, or when the highest authorized scope (including an epic, feature, rollout, or standalone outcome) is complete. ([redducklabs/law-di-gras](https://github.com/redducklabs/law-di-gras/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-handoff) · 2 copies)
- **agent-handoff**: Valide un message de passage entre agents ou le rapport final d'un agent (JSON Schema, fichiers cités présents) et le note au journal d'un workflow ; donne la liste compacte des agents et workflows classés pour une demande. À utiliser par tout agent avant de passer la main à un autre ou de rendre son rapport final, et pour choisir par où commencer. ([RenaudVanhille/myskills](https://github.com/RenaudVanhille/myskills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Pass work between coding agents (Claude Code, Antigravity, Codex) through a brief file in the project's `.handoff/` folder, so work moves between apps with no paste when Antigravity's own watcher sidecar is on, or one pasted line otherwise. Three modes - out (write a brief for another agent and print the line to paste), in (read the brief addressed to me, check it against git, take the writer lock, continue), return (write the result back, mark the brief done, release the lock). Use when the user says "hand this to Antigravity/Codex/Claude", "continue what Claude/Antigravity started", "pick u… ([RitikBhardwaj-programmer/claude-setup](https://github.com/RitikBhardwaj-programmer/claude-setup/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: 多 Agent 兩階段會話彙整與接手工具。當使用者要求產出或讀取 HANDOFF.md、交接 Claude/Codex/Cursor/Antigravity/Hermes 工作，或匯入舊 Claude Code session 時使用。 ([SanHsien/cc-session-reader](https://github.com/SanHsien/cc-session-reader/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Prepare for the next agent or session by documenting work and leaving codebase in good state ([SebEdena/agents](https://github.com/SebEdena/agents/tree/HEAD/core/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff** ([serdevir91/ai-skills-bundle](https://github.com/serdevir91/ai-skills-bundle/tree/HEAD/skills/community/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Keep a project conversation continuous across Claude Code and Codex. Use "catch up" at the start of a session (or when switching tools) to load the shared project memory, the handoff log, and what the other agent last did in this folder; use "handoff" at the end of a substantial work block to record state, decisions, and next steps in the shared memory. Triggers - catch up, where were we, what did Codex/Claude do, switch to Claude/Codex, handoff, wrap up, save state for the other agent. ([sfuchs-de/coauthor-skills](https://github.com/sfuchs-de/coauthor-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Create, validate, and resume structured handoff documents that let another AI agent continue work without hidden conversation context. Use when pausing or transferring unfinished work, approaching a context limit, switching agents or sessions, reporting a blocker, recovering after interruption, resuming from HANDOFF.md, or auditing a handoff for evidence, actionable next steps, secret leakage, and unsupported completion claims. ([sonu07-star/agent-handoff-skill](https://github.com/sonu07-star/agent-handoff-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Protocol for agents receiving work from the @story orchestrator: intake checks, context recovery, progress tracking, and MCP fallback. Read when picking up a handoff or starting a multi-step task. ([sthirion/pantry-pal](https://github.com/sthirion/pantry-pal/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Gabarits pour transmettre le travail entre agents sans perte - le brief qu'on donne à un agent, le compte rendu qu'il rend, la reprise après une décision humaine ou un refus. À utiliser par l'orchestrateur ou toute session qui lance un agent, et par un agent qui rend la main. ([tchamouramses/Agent-Prompt-For-Developper](https://github.com/tchamouramses/Agent-Prompt-For-Developper/tree/HEAD/Skills/Global/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Seamless handoff protocol between agents. Defines the Context Bundle that is passed forward so the developer never re-enters information, and the single handoff banner shown at each transition. Loaded by orchestrator and every phase agent. ([trackmind-ai/sdlc-atlas](https://github.com/trackmind-ai/sdlc-atlas/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-atlas/platform/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Create durable handoff memory and task notes for agent swarms, long sessions, and resumed work while avoiding private/runtime state. ([victoryforphil/skill_issue](https://github.com/victoryforphil/skill_issue/tree/HEAD/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Produce concise handoff notes for another agent or developer, including changed files, verification, risks, and next PR slice. ([visomi-dev/visomi-stack](https://github.com/visomi-dev/visomi-stack/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-handoff) · 2 copies)
- **agent-handoff**: Write the handoff record ("交件單") that ends an agent's round in an implement–review–finish loop, so the next step can be decided by a script instead of by reading the conversation. Use at the end of an implementation round, a review round, or a completion run when the Change has a loop (`.agent-handoff/&lt;change-id&gt;/loop.yaml`), or whenever the user asks to leave a handoff record. Records are written only through the bundled `handoff-write` script. ([wkchen5872/wk-agent-ops](https://github.com/wkchen5872/wk-agent-ops/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-handoff) · 3 copies)
- **agent-handoff**: 在 Codex, Claude 或其他 Agent 交替推进同一项目时, 核对工作树, 记录进度并交接执行者. 也用于按需查看前一执行者的本地会话记录. ([x380kkm/harness-manager](https://github.com/x380kkm/harness-manager/tree/HEAD/src/harness_manager/bundled/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff**: Produces a typed, structured handoff packet with base SHA, artifact digests, open questions, and explicit escalation so another agent can continue safely; use when asked to "write a handoff", "hand off to the next agent", "pass this to review", "continue in another session", or when work crosses an agent, role, or session boundary. ([yoosuf/ae.sh](https://github.com/yoosuf/ae.sh/tree/HEAD/framework/skills/agent-handoff))
- **agent-handoff-brief**: Use when handing work from one AI agent, developer, or session to another by summarizing current state, files touched, decisions made, blockers, verification, and next safe actions. ([alexzhu0/agent-ready-skills](https://github.com/alexzhu0/agent-ready-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff-brief))
- **agent-handoff-brief-composition**: Compose structured handoff briefs between agents that transfer goals, constraints, in-flight state, and verification obligations without loss or silent reinterpretation. ([SamoTech/skills-tree](https://github.com/SamoTech/skills-tree/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/agent-handoff-brief-composition) · ★4)
- **agent-handoff-briefs**: Use when turning a ramble into a one-shot Codex prompt. ([prithvi-jpg/cloud-agents](https://github.com/prithvi-jpg/cloud-agents/tree/HEAD/portable/custom/agent-handoff-briefs) · ★1)
- **agent-handoff-commit**: Manage persistent multi-agent context handoffs (in-place updates to docs/agents/STATE.md and DECISIONS.md), AST symbol lookups, memory byte-budget checks, and mandatory per-request Git commits in e:\ai-video-upscaler. - **agent-handoff-context**: Use when creating an agent handoff, context file, continuation note, or execution brief for another agent to continue work. ([EternalGoldenBraid/.dots](https://github.com/EternalGoldenBraid/.dots/tree/HEAD/agents/copilot/skills/agent-handoff-context))
- **agent-handoff-context**: Use when you need to contextualizar rápido a un agente nuevo, revisar si el agente anterior quedó con procesos sin terminar y verificar si el repo tiene cambios sin subir a Git. - **agent-handoff-continuation**: Agent Handoff Continuation Workflow: Use when work may continue across turns, interruptions, context compaction, another agent, or a handoff back to the user. - **agent-handoff-contract**: Agent Handoff Contract template, required fields definition, and acceptance criteria. - **agent-handoff-design**: **Purpose**: Design effective handoffs between agents in multi-agent workflows to ensure seamless information transfer, maintain context continuity, and maximize overall system success. - **agent-handoff-elicitation**: 前任Agentからプロジェクトや作業を引き継ぐための高密度なハンドオフ情報を引き出すスキル。別Agent・別スレッド・別セッションからの引き継ぎ、コンテキスト圧縮前後の継続、作業交代、途中状態の整理、前任者への「何を聞けばいいか」の作成で必ず使う。単なる要約ではなく、目的、未完了事項、判断理由、検証状況、失敗した探索、リスク、次の一手を受け手Agent目線で抽出する。 ([yharuto0917/yharuto-agent-skills](https://github.com/yharuto0917/yharuto-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-handoff-elicitation))
- **agent-handoff-evidence-best-practices**: Route multi-agent work with durable intake, handoff, review, and test artifacts. - **agent-handoff-governance**: gobernar handoffs entre agentes y subagentes para que el trabajo delegado conserve contexto mínimo suficiente, owner explícito, criterio de aceptación, eta y trazabilidad hasta volver al solicitante. usar cuando un agente detecta bloqueo, necesita experiencia especializada, debe decidir entre subagente vs issue vs update, registrar el traspaso en linear y/o notion, evitar `no_reply` o cierres prematuros, auditar delegaciones, integrar resultados de subagentes o distinguir progreso real de fake progress.
